# Phase 4b — delete the legacy libraries, retire the dotted keys, widen the lint

**Date:** 2026-10-22 · **Status:** PLAN, written on `e2d7a8f` (Phase 4a
integrated, the tree clean). The parallel packages of §4 build against it; the
integrator fills §8.1 and §11. **Re-planned 2026-10-23** on the working tree:
waves 0–3 (P0–P9) have landed UNCOMMITTED on top of `e2d7a8f` (the 621 legacy
files deleted, the aliases, lockfile, i18n succession, translator, lint
widening and docs done); a fresh re-derivation found the residue §4.R plans as
waves 4 (R1–R8) and 5 (the integrator). Do not redo P0–P9. **Revised
2026-10-23** (second review): residue rows h–n, R2 widened to every
legacy-dotted error payload, R3 gains the flatten-spec status and three
runbook gates, R4's text corrected, R5–R8 added; each gap re-verified on
the tree. **Integrated 2026-10-23** (wave 5, §8.1): waves 0–4 assembled
(R4 applied afterwards on the owner's go-ahead), the static half of the HANDOFF §4 gate green
(baseline reds only), runbook §4.2–§4.5, §4.7 and §4.8 PASS, the functions
deps re-recorded. **Gate green 2026-10-23** (second pass, §8.1 item 9):
the FULL HANDOFF §4 gate re-run after `nx reset` — baseline reds only,
`verify:p0` all proved, `test:emulator` 65 + 255, `e2e:web` 19 failed / 41
passed (the baseline) — and the §8 step 6 dev smoke 35 checks, 0 failed.
EXECUTED; R4 applied 2026-10-23 on the owner's go-ahead for Phase 4 (the
orchestrator, after the workflow), and the "Phase 4b" commit landed. ·
**Law:** `CLAUDE.md` Laws 1–9 and the eight `.claude/rules/*.md`. This plan
never overrides them. ·
**Sources:** `docs/architecture/HANDOFF.md` (§3.3 "Phase 4b — what it must
do", §4 gate and baselines, §5 operating rules, §6 rulings);
`docs/architecture/flips/phase-4a-repoint.md` (§0 4A-5, 4A-13, 4A-15, §8.1
item 9 (g), §10.1, §11.3 the 4b pre-ledger); `docs/architecture/ecs-migration.md`
§6 Phase 4 (deletions, "Nx", the gate); `docs/architecture/firestore-runbook.md`
§3, §4.0–§4.7. Every site below was re-derived with `git grep` on `e2d7a8f`;
line numbers are hints on that commit and drift.

**What 4b is.** A pure deletion and re-wording. 4a left nothing outside
`libs/application/**` and `libs/domain/**` importing them (runbook §4.7 PASS),
so 4b deletes the 17 Nx projects with every reference to them, drops `luxon`,
`@types/luxon` and `dinero.js`, retires the dotted i18n keys after giving the
still-read ones SCREAMING successors with today's words, deletes
`translateDomainError`'s `{ code }` arm and `translateFunctionsError`, widens
the Phase 1 project-scoped ECS lint blocks to the root, lands the end-of-3b
graph/bundle assertion as code, and rewrites the docs so the 3-tier shape reads
as removed. **4b ends** when no path, alias, tag, package or dotted key of the
legacy shape survives outside historical docs, the full gate of HANDOFF §4 is
green (baseline reds only) and runbook §4.2–§4.8 PASS.

Greenfield, as before: no fallback reader, no dual key, no shim. Nothing is
committed by a package; the integrator owns the gate.

---

## 0. Decisions this plan takes (read first)

| #     | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Why                                                                     |
| ----- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 4B-1  | **Frozen behaviour.** Every outcome a person sees or a request leaves with equals today's: every screen, every message in `bg` and `en` byte for byte, every DOM test id and `data-state`, every callable status and `details`, every stored document, every telemetry line. A dotted key is retired ONLY after its SCREAMING successor holds the same words in both locales and every reader reads the successor. Swapping `translateDomainError(t, { code: X })` for `translateDomainError(t, { errorCode: X })` cannot change an outcome: both arms look up `errors.<X>` and fall back to the raw `X`; the only difference is the params (the struct itself instead of `params`), and no catalogue string interpolates `{{errorCode}}` (0 hits in either locale). **Departures: none.**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | HANDOFF §5; 4A-1.                                                       |
| 4B-2  | **What is deleted.** (1) The 17 Nx projects under `libs/application/*` (accounts, booking, catalog, engagement, governance, identity, notifications, shared) and `libs/domain/*` (accounts, catalog, engagement, governance, identity, kernel, models, notifications, scheduling): 621 tracked files, 157 `*.spec.ts` + 12 `*.type-spec.ts`; their 19 `tsconfig.base.json` aliases (incl. `@creativo/application/engagement/testing`, whose file 4a already deleted), 17 `.nxignore` lines, the `type:application` depConstraints row and its three mentions. (2) `luxon`, `@types/luxon`, `dinero.js` from `package.json` and the lockfile, with the dinero cross-check describe of `money.data.spec.ts` (its properties re-targeted, 4B-6) and its lint exemption. (3) `translateFunctionsError`, `FunctionsErrorDetails`, `UNKNOWN_ERROR_KEY`, the `{ code, params }` member of `CodedError` and its branch, and the i18n lib's only `firebase/functions` import. (4) Every lowercase-rooted leaf under `errors` in both catalogues (139 per locale on `e2d7a8f`: the 13 still read, after their successors land, and 126 with no reader). (5) `docs/architecture/domain-model.md` (D6).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Blueprint §6 Phase 4; HANDOFF §3.3 4b 1–5.                              |
| 4B-3  | **What is NOT deleted (already gone, or out of 4b).** `LegacyWireCode`, `LEGACY_WIRE_CODE`, `LEGACY_WIRE_CODE_BY_CODE` were deleted in 4a (4A-4); only comments name them. The Phase 2 shim bridges lived inside the legacy classes (and `libs/application/identity/src/lib/registration-fields.shim.ts`) and die with the directories. `libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/visit-ecs.shim.ts` and the frozen visit view models stay (4A-13: the view-model pass). The flip-1 ports with no caller (`markNotificationRead`, `loadWaitlistRequest`, `loadCouponGrant`, `readCustomers`, `CouponGrantSystem.transitionWrite`) stay (they wait for grant burn/restore). All 37 tokens of `ports.tokens.ts` and every export of the 25 infrastructure `*.adapter.ts` have a production consumer: no dead port, token or adapter outside the legacy dirs is deleted. `legacy-wire-successors.spec.ts` stays (it pins SCREAMING successors in their dictionaries; it reads no legacy code). `LEGACY_OTP_DESTINATION_TYPE` (`console-otp-sender.adapter.ts`) is an operator log format, frozen. The `dinero.js`/`luxon` entries of `LAYER_IMPORT_RESTRICTIONS` and of the functions' `IMPORT_RESTRICTIONS` STAY as retired-package guards (removing them weakens a rule); only their messages lose the kernel clauses. `rxjs` stays in the functions dist (4a §10.1, 4A-2 (d)).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Scope; "never weaken a rule".                                           |
| 4B-4  | **How each still-read dotted key is succeeded** (§2 is the table). Mechanical rule: the successor is a SCREAMING, domain-prefixed `errorCode` declared ONCE as a value of the matching primitives dictionary (Law 7.5) — `ACCOUNTS_UPLOAD_AVATAR_UPLOAD_FAILED`, `ACCOUNTS_REMOVE_AVATAR_REMOVE_FAILED`, `ACCOUNTS_UPDATE_PROFILE_REPOSITORY_FAILURE`, `ACCOUNTS_REPOSITORY_FAILURE` (client account), `IDENTITY_IDENTIFIER_MISSING`, `IDENTITY_CALL_FAILED` (the auth/onboarding generic, today's `errors.unknown`), `ENGAGEMENT_REPOSITORY_FAILURE` (admin rhythm), `BOOKING_CALL_FAILED` (today's `booking.gateway.failed`, four readers), `BOOKING_TRANSITION_SEAT_RESOLVED` — plus five flat reason-level keys `PHONE_INVALID.<reason>` that need no new code, keyed by the `PhoneInvalidReason` dictionary's OWN values (`PHONE_INVALID.too-short`, nothing maps `-` → `_`) and read through one new translator rule — `translateDomainError(t, error, reason)`'s optional third argument looks up `errors.<errorCode>.<reason>` — so the struct keeps its real dictionary `errorCode` (`PHONE_INVALID`, Law 7.5) exactly as `wordPipelineError`'s `serverCopyCodes` builds `<code>.<reason>` from a struct that keeps its own code. Each key holds today's words verbatim in both locales. **No twin is reused across meaning:** `errors.unknown` ≠ `PIPELINE_UNKNOWN` (dash vs full stop); `booking.gateway.failed` = `PIPELINE_UNKNOWN` in words but 4A-5 keeps each surface's generic key its own; `errors.repository_failure` = `IDENTITY_REPOSITORY_FAILURE` in words but not in domain. **Traps avoided:** no value is added to `PipelineErrorCode` (enumerated in production: `PIPELINE_CODES`), `RepositoryErrorCode` (enumerated in production: `wire-read.ts` `STORE_FAILURES`) or `StreakPublishErrorCode` (`admin-rhythm.store.ts` `PUBLISH_REFUSALS`) — each would change a classification; `seat_resolved` is a FLAT code, never `<serverErrorCode>.seat_resolved` (`move.system.ts` raises `BookingError.invalidInput('seat_resolved')`, which `wordPipelineError` would then word). | Law 7.5; 4A-5; reuse only where meaning matches.                        |
| 4B-5  | **Every new code gets an inert `EDGE_WIRE` row.** `EDGE_WIRE` is typed total over every code of every primitives dictionary and `edge.spec.ts` proves it ("covers every error dictionary ecs-core declares"). The nine new codes are web-side: no pipeline raises them. Each gets `fields(S.Internal)` with no named fields — the same answer the edge gives a code it does not know, so even a hypothetical raise could not change an outcome (precedent: `PIPELINE_WIRE`, the web-only classification, has rows).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Law 7.5 + the edge's totality rule; OQ-3.                               |
| 4B-6  | **The money cross-check keeps its strength.** In `money.data.spec.ts` the describe «Money agrees with dinero.js (property)» loses its dinero import, NOT its properties: add, subtract and sum are checked against exact `BigInt` arithmetic on the same random EUR amounts; the decimal-factor scale in every rounding mode against the file's exact `reference(BigInt(a) * BigInt(factor), 10n ** BigInt(decimals), mode)` — today `reference` is local to the integer-ratio `it` (:429), so it is HOISTED once to module scope and shared by both properties (that hoist is the only change to the integer-ratio case, recorded in §11.2); the «scale by any integer ratio matches exact rational rounding» case (it never used dinero) moves out of the describe. Same generators, same run counts. Because both properties then check one in-file oracle, an independent pin replaces dinero: BEFORE P6 removes the package, P3 captures dinero's own answers for a fixed table of tie and sign vectors (each of the 7 modes × a positive and a negative factor × decimals 1–4, exact ties included) with a throwaway scratchpad script, and commits them as a golden `it.each` that imports no dinero.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | "Never weaken a test".                                                  |
| 4B-7  | **Lint widening** (§3 is the table). The root replaces `ecsSyntaxBlocks(ECS_ROOT_SUFFIXES)` with `ecsSyntaxBlocks(ECS_ALL_SUFFIXES, { extra: ECS_TOKEN_RESTRICTIONS })`: the `*.port.ts` and `*.adapter.ts` blocks and the `InjectionToken` guard on every ECS-suffix file of the workspace. The root also gains `files: ['**/*.port.ts']` → `'creativo/no-sliced-ports': ['error', { anyType: true }]`; libs/ecs-core KEEPS its `src/ports/**` strict block (moving it would narrow coverage: the ports barrel and the ports specs are not `*.port.ts`). The token guard is NOT installed on every root `*.ts`: it would flag 67 lawful Angular-wiring sites in 15 files (`ports.tokens.ts` ×37, `firebase-app.provider.ts`, `lazy-port.ts` + specs, `app.config.ts`, the `libs/ui/*` tokens, feature rigs) — Law 4 allows `InjectionToken` in Angular UI wiring, so the every-file guard stays project-scoped in libs/ecs-core and apps/functions (`ecsProjectBlocks`), where no Angular lives. Measured read-only with the ESLint API on `e2d7a8f`: the widened blocks flag 0 of the 63 non-legacy `*.port.ts`/`*.adapter.ts`, and do flag the legacy ports (the control). `ECS_ROOT_SUFFIXES` ("the five suffixes no legacy file carries") is retired. The `type:application` depConstraints row and tag go; the type:ui→domain, util, tokens, tooling rows stay (blueprint §6 Phase 4 "Nx"; D10(a): `type:domain` is ecs-core only). The i18n project's firebase exemption goes with its last firebase import (a tightening).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Blueprint §6 Phase 4 "Nx"; Law 4; HANDOFF §3.3 4b 4.                    |
| 4B-8  | **Deps changes.** `package.json` loses `@types/luxon`, `dinero.js`, `luxon`; `pnpm install` drops their lockfile entries (9 lines on `e2d7a8f`, all direct, no transitive dependent). The functions dist then lists `firebase-admin`, `firebase-functions`, `libphonenumber-js`, `rxjs`; the integrator re-records `tools/verify/p0/functions-deps.baseline.json` AFTER the last lint-config change (a config that imported `tools/lint-rules` directly instead of `#eslint-config` would add a graph edge and grow the dist). `functions-deps.mjs` gains a forbidden-key check (no `@angular/` prefix, no `luxon`, no `dinero.js`) so the end-of-3b assertion is code, not prose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | 4A-15; blueprint end-of-3b.                                             |
| 4B-9  | **Provenance stays, the paths go.** Code and its comments name no `libs/application`/`libs/domain` path, alias or `domain/kernel` (≈93 lines in 83 ecs-core files + 9 elsewhere on `e2d7a8f`): a case-ledger header "The cases of `libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/seat.spec.ts`, copied over" becomes "The cases of the legacy scheduling `seat.spec.ts` (deleted in Phase 4b; ledger `docs/architecture/flips/phase-4b-delete.md` §11.1)". The trail moves to §11.1 (a historical doc) before the files go. Past-tense provenance naming a retired NAME (`successor of the legacy wordError`) stays; a present-tense claim that is now false ("the edge still sends the legacy dotted code until Phase 4", "the legacy shims convert", "deleted in 4b" in the future tense) is rewritten.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | The 4b grep gate; HANDOFF §3.3 4b 5.                                    |
| 4B-10 | **What stays and why (non-legacy look-alikes).** `type:domain` (= libs/ecs-core, D10(a)); the `@creativo/features/marketing/landing/testing` alias (the `TESTING_ENTRY_POINT_RESTRICTION` example re-points to it); `docs/architecture/domain-deviations.md`, `docs/migration-blueprint.md`, `docs/migration/goals/*.md`, the flip plans and the 4a plan — historical, bannered, not deleted under D6; the design-research specs that still cite `libs/(application `staff-schedule-uiux-design.md`, `staff-surfaces-proposal.md`, `staff-visit-editor-design.md`) — live specs whose legacy links become dead history: each gets a one-line banner (P9), none is rewritten; ### 1.3 Readers of the dotted keys (the complete census)

Scanned every lowercase-rooted `errors` leaf as `errors.<key>` and as a quoted
`'<key>'` over `libs/**/*.{ts,html}`, `apps/**/*.{ts,html}` and `tools/**`, plus
every dynamic `errors.${…}` / `'errors.' +` construction (only
`translate-domain-error.ts`). Production readers:

| Key (under `errors`)                                                       | Readers                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `accounts.upload_avatar.upload_failed`                                     | `avatar-refusal.ts` `AVATAR_UPLOAD_FAILED` → `profile.store.ts`, `onboarding-flow.store.ts` → `translateError(code)` of `client-profile.ts`, `client-onboarding.ts`                                                                                            |
| `accounts.remove_avatar.remove_failed`                                     | `avatar-refusal.ts` `AVATAR_REMOVE_FAILED` → `profile.store.ts` → `client-profile.ts`                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `accounts.update_profile.repository_failure`                               | `own-profile.ts` `PROFILE_WRITE_FAILED` → `profile.store.ts`, `onboarding-flow.store.ts` (and `booking-contact-sheet.ts`, which only checks `null`)                                                                                                            |
| `identifier_missing`                                                       | `onboarding-flow.store.ts` `IDENTIFIER_MISSING`, `profile.store.ts` `IDENTIFIER_MISSING`                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `unknown`                                                                  | `auth-flow.store.ts` `UNKNOWN_ERROR_CODE` → `client-auth.ts`; `onboarding-flow.store.ts` `UNKNOWN_ERROR_CODE` → `client-onboarding.ts`; `translate-domain-error.ts` `UNKNOWN_ERROR_KEY` (deleted with `translateFunctionsError`)                               |
| `repository_failure`                                                       | `admin-rhythm.ts` ×3 (`{ code: 'repository_failure' }` — the toast on a non-refusal publish and undo failure, and `wordRefusal`); `client-account.ts` `translateRepositoryError` (`client-account.html`)                                                       |
| `identity.phone.{not_a_number,too_short,too_long,invalid_country,invalid}` | `client-onboarding.ts` `phoneError` (dynamic, with `{{example}}`)                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `booking.gateway.failed`                                                   | `booking-error-message.ts` `REVIEW_FALLBACK_CODE`; `client-appointments.ts` `CANCEL_FAILED`; `staff-dashboard.ts` `refuse()`'s default `fallbackKey = 'errors.booking.gateway.failed'` (a direct translate) and `describeError`'s `wordPipelineError` fallback |
| `booking.transition.seat_resolved`                                         | `staff-dashboard.ts` `describeError` (a direct translate)                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

The `{ code }` callers of `translateDomainError` are exactly `admin-rhythm.ts`
(×3), `client-account.ts`, `client-auth.ts` `translateError`,
`client-onboarding.ts` `translateError` and `phoneError`, `client-profile.ts`
`translateError`. Every other caller already passes `{ errorCode }`.
`translateFunctionsError` has no caller.

Пробвай пак.                                       | `booking-error-message.ts` `REVIEW_FALLBACK_CODE`; `client-appointments.ts` `CANCEL_FAILED`; `staff-dashboard.ts` `refuse()` default `fallbackKey` (→ `` `errors.${BookingErrorCode.CallFailed}` ``) and `describeError`'s fallback (P2) |

Notes. (a) The phone keys are FLAT keys at the `errors` level
(`"PHONE_INVALID.too-short": "…"`), because `errors.PHONE_INVALID` is already a
string leaf (its own words, `{{attempted}}`); a nested object would collide.
The reason segment is the `PhoneInvalidReason` value itself (kebab), so no
use site maps `-` → `_` (today's `client-onboarding.ts` `phoneError` does; the
mapping dies with it). `wordPipelineError` can never build `PHONE_INVALID.<x>`:
the `PHONE_INVALID` wire allowlist is `attempted`, `reason` — neither is
`refusal` nor `field`. The struct stays honest (Law 7.5: its `errorCode` is a
dictionary value): the reader passes the real code and the reason separately —
`translateDomainError(t, { errorCode: PhoneErrorCode.Invalid, example }, reason)` —
and the ONE rule that builds `errors.<errorCode>.<reason>` (falling back to the
raw `<errorCode>.<reason>`) lives in `translate-domain-error.ts`, beside the
lookup it extends (P0).
(b) `catalogue-parity.spec.ts` demands the same leaves and the same params in
both locales: add each key to `en.json` and `bg.json` together. (c) The other
126 lowercase-rooted leaves (the legacy `DomainError` codes, `money.*`,
`time.*`, `phone.*`, the `identity`/`accounts` subtrees, `registration_*`,
`booking.{flow,waitlist,commit,staffEdit,cancel,reschedule,contact,transition}.*`,
`scheduling.*`, `empty_id` … ) have no reader since the 4a wire flip (each has a
SCREAMING twin or is dead): P5 deletes every lowercase-rooted child of `errors`
in one edit, in both files.

| --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| `InjectionToken` guard (`ECS_TOKEN_RESTRICTIONS`) — every file of ecs-core and functions                                                                                    | on the seven suffix blocks only (the `extra`); the every-file half stays project-scoped (`ecsProjectBlocks`) | 0 on suffix files                                                                                                                                                                                                        | the every-file root guard is NOT installed: 67 lawful Angular-wiring sites in 15 files (Law 4), the stated reason                                                                                                                        |
| `creativo/no-sliced-ports` strict (`{ anyType: true }`) — ecs-core `src/ports/**`                                                                                           | a root block `files: ['**/*.port.ts']`; ecs-core keeps `src/ports/**`                                        | 0                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| i18n's firebase exemption                                                                                                                                                   | deleted (the root `LAYER_IMPORT_RESTRICTIONS` applies whole)                                                 | 0 once `translate-domain-error.ts` drops `firebase/functions`                                                                                                                                                            | none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `money.data.spec.ts`' `dinero.js` exemption                                                                                                                                 | deleted                                                                                                      | 0 once the spec imports no dinero                                                                                                                                                                                        | none                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `LAYER_IMPORT_RESTRICTIONS` `dinero.js`/`luxon` bans; functions' `IMPORT_RESTRICTIONS`                                                                                      | KEPT (retired-package guards)                                                                                | 0                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | messages lose "(only the legacy libs/domain/kernel/** keeps …, until Phase 4)" and "(and the legacy libs/domain/kernel/** until Phase 4)"; the infrastructure configs' comment "keeping the dinero/luxon/libphonenumber half" stays true |

Any other hit the widened blocks raise is FIXED in the flagged file (the owning
package of §4, or the integrator), never exempted; an exemption needs a stated
reason in §8.1. The functions config keeps importing through `#eslint-config`.

**Nested configs that cancel the root blocks (added 2026-10-23, R8).** Flat
config does not merge a rule's options: a later block that sets
`no-restricted-syntax` replaces every earlier one for the files it matches.
`libs/ui/icons/eslint.config.mjs` turns `no-restricted-syntax` `'off'` on
`**/*.ts` after spreading the root (the registry boundary — glyph names and
vector data live there), which silently drops the seven root ECS suffix
blocks for any `*.{system,data,entity,primitive,pipeline,port,adapter}.ts`
in that project (measured: `calculateConfigForFile('src/lib/probe.data.ts')`
→ severity 0, no `ClassDeclaration`). No such file exists there today, so
nothing is flagged; R8 scopes the `'off'` away from the suffixes
(`ignores`) so the root blocks reach them, and §6 gate 9 gains an icons probe.
`tools/icons` and `tools/lint-rules` turn the rule off only on `**/*.mjs`
(their only `.ts` files are the ignored `__fixtures__`), which the `.ts`
suffix blocks never match — not a gap. `libs/ecs-core` restates the rule
through `ecsProjectBlocks` (stricter, not weaker).

**Why the class guard stays suffix-scoped, not every-file.** `class` is legal
in the Angular row (CLAUDE.md §2), and Angular classes live in files of many
names (`*.component.ts`, `*.service.ts`, `*.page.ts`, `app.ts`,
`frame-fullscreen.ts` with `inject`/`signal`), so an every-file root guard
would flag the lawful UI. The ECS suffixes carry it; `libs/ecs-core` and
`apps/functions` carry it on every file (`ecsProjectBlocks`, no Angular
there). The undecorated non-Angular classes outside those reaches are named
and carried forward in §4.R row n and HANDOFF (none is new in 4b).

## 4. Packages

Ownership is disjoint WITHIN a wave; a file may be owned by different packages
in different waves (the earlier wave hands it over finished). Every package
sweeps the provenance (4B-9) of the files it owns. Nobody commits, pushes,
checks out, stashes or resets; undo is the inverse edit. Every package runs
its own projects' `typecheck` (`--skip-nx-cache`), `lint` and `test` before
handing over; only the integrator runs the emulator journeys.

### P0 — the successor vocabulary · wave 0

**Files:** `libs/ecs-core/src/primitives/{accounts,identity,booking,engagement,phone}-errors.data.ts` and their `.data.spec.ts`;
`libs/ecs-core/src/components/{booking-core,identity,loyalty}-dictionaries.type-spec.ts` (only if the compiler demands);
`apps/functions/src/edge.ts`, `apps/functions/src/edge.spec.ts`;
`libs/infrastructure/i18n/src/assets/i18n/en.json`, `bg.json`;
`libs/infrastructure/i18n/src/lib/translate-domain-error.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) — the
`reason` argument only (P5 owns both files again in wave 2).

**Brief.** (0) `translateDomainError(transloco, error, reason?: string)`: with a
`reason`, the key is `errors.<errorCode>.<reason>` and the raw fallback
`<errorCode>.<reason>`; without one, nothing changes. Spec: a case with the
real catalogue (after (5)) and one with an unknown reason (raw fallback). The
`{ code }` arm is untouched here (P5 deletes it). (1) Add the nine codes of §2 to their dictionaries, each with JSDoc
saying which surface words it and that no pipeline raises it; follow each
file's own pattern (a variant in its one type-only namespace + a factory where
every other code has one — `booking-errors.data.spec.ts`' «every code built by a
factory» case needs `BookingError.bare(...)` to accept the two booking codes).
(2) Extend each dictionary spec: the code lists of `booking-errors.data.spec.ts`
(the sorted list and the factory list), `accounts-`, `identity-`,
`engagement-errors.data.spec.ts`; a case per new code pinning its spelling.
(3) `phone-errors.data.spec.ts` «keeps the kernel reason strings, which map 1:1
onto errors.identity.phone.* keys» → same intent, the subject
`PHONE_INVALID.<reason>` (the value itself, no mapping); `phone-errors.data.ts` doc :19 likewise.
(4) `edge.ts`: one row each, `fields(S.Internal)`, no named field —
`ACCOUNTS_WIRE` ×4, `IDENTITY_WIRE` ×2, `BOOKING_WIRE` ×2, and an override in
`ENGAGEMENT_WIRE`'s `uniform` (its default is `invalid-argument`). `edge.spec.ts`
changes only if a count or table pins the dictionaries' size. (5) Add the 14
keys of §2 to BOTH catalogues with the words verbatim; do NOT delete any dotted
key (P5, wave 2). Edit the catalogues AS TEXT (insert the lines), never by
`JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(…))`: a round-trip rewrites the files' `’` and
` ` escapes as literal characters (9 en and 7 bg lines outside `errors`
on `e2d7a8f`, the NBSP invisibly). Prove equality: a node one-liner comparing
each successor with its retired key in both files must print nothing, and a
value-level check (every leaf that existed before has an identical parsed
value after, both locales) plus `git diff` showing only `+` lines (record both
in the hand-over). (6) Never touch `PipelineErrorCode`, `RepositoryErrorCode`,
`StreakPublishErrorCode` (4B-4). (7) Sweep the provenance of the five
dictionary files (accounts/identity/engagement docs name `libs/domain/*`;
`identity-errors.data.ts`'s "the edge still sends the legacy dotted code on
the wire until Phase 4" and `booking-errors.data.ts`/`engagement-errors.data.ts`'s
"until Phase 4" are false since 4a). Gate: `ecs-core` and `functions`
typecheck/test/lint, `i18n` typecheck/lint/test (catalogue parity, the `reason` cases).

### P1 — identity and profile surfaces re-pointed · wave 1

**Files:** `libs/features/shared/shell/src/lib/identity/avatar-refusal.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`);
`libs/features/client/account-state/src/lib/own-profile.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`);
`libs/features/client/profile/src/lib/profile.store.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`), `client-profile/client-profile.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`);
`libs/features/client/onboarding/src/lib/onboarding-flow.store.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`), `client-onboarding/client-onboarding.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`);
`libs/features/client/auth/src/lib/auth-flow.store.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`), `client-auth/client-auth.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`);
`libs/features/client/account/src/lib/client-account/client-account.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`).

**Brief.** FIRST, on today's tree, the missing pins (§5.3; green before any
re-point, so they prove 4B-1 across the change): `client-account.spec.ts` — a
failed read renders the client-account error state with today's words (en
«Something went wrong on our end — please try again.» where the rig loads the
real catalogue; otherwise the key under the echo loader, re-targeted with the
re-point and recorded in §11.2); `onboarding-flow.store.spec.ts` — a missing
owner gives `state.error === 'identifier_missing'` and a code-less failed
register gives `state.error === 'unknown'` (re-targeted with the re-point to
`IdentityErrorCode.IdentifierMissing` and `IdentityErrorCode.CallFailed`,
recorded in §11.2).
THEN re-point §2 rows 1–6 and 8–12. The three EXPORTED constants keep their
names because they have importers (`profile.store.ts`, `avatar-refusal.spec.ts`,
`own-profile.spec.ts`) and become their dictionary value
(`AVATAR_UPLOAD_FAILED = AccountsErrorCode.UploadAvatarUploadFailed`,
`AVATAR_REMOVE_FAILED = AccountsErrorCode.RemoveAvatarRemoveFailed`,
`PROFILE_WRITE_FAILED = AccountsErrorCode.UpdateProfileRepositoryFailure`);
the four module-private aliases (`auth-flow.store.ts:27` and
`onboarding-flow.store.ts:53` `UNKNOWN_ERROR_CODE`, `onboarding-flow.store.ts:50`
and `profile.store.ts:21` `IDENTIFIER_MISSING`) are DELETED and their use
sites read `IdentityErrorCode.CallFailed` / `IdentityErrorCode.IdentifierMissing`
directly (the dictionary is the only source of the string — reuse, never
copy). Every `translateDomainError(t, { code })` becomes `{ errorCode: code }`;
`phoneError` passes the real code and the reason separately —
`translateDomainError(this.transloco, { errorCode: PhoneErrorCode.Invalid, example }, reason)`
(P0's `reason` argument; the `-` → `_` mapping is deleted, §2 note (a));
`client-account.ts` passes `{ errorCode: AccountsErrorCode.RepositoryFailure }`
(NOT `UpdateProfileRepositoryFailure`, whose words differ).
Re-target the specs that pin the old strings (same intent, new subject):
`avatar-refusal.spec.ts:31`, `profile.store.spec.ts:156,169,202,259`,
`onboarding-flow.store.spec.ts:225`, `client-onboarding.spec.ts:394-396` (the
empty-catalogue raw fallback becomes `PHONE_INVALID.too-short`) and `:617`,

/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9]*(?:_[A-Z0-9]+)*$/;` and its one use (:132) with an equivalent
check that has no nested quantifier: `const SCREAMING_CHARS =
/^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]*$/;` and a named predicate `isScreamingSnake(code) =
SCREAMING_CHARS.test(code) && !code.includes('__') && !code.endsWith('_')` —
the same language (a leading letter, `_`-separated non-empty segments). No
`eslint-disable`. Add no case; the strength is unchanged. **Gate:** `pnpm nx
run i18n:lint` → 0 problems (warnings included); `pnpm nx run i18n:test
--skip-nx-cache` → 5 files / 66 tests, as before; `typecheck`. Report the
file's case count (unchanged) for §11.2.

(+ `.spec.ts`), `libs/features/client/account-state/src/lib/own-profile.ts`

with one comment line why (an ECS-suffix file here keeps the root's suffix

**Gate:** from `libs/ui/icons`,
`new ESLint().calculateConfigForFile('src/lib/probe.data.ts')` shows

libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-card.format.spec.ts` and

## 5. Specs

### 5.1 Deleted

- **The 169 legacy spec files** die with their directories (§1.1; ≈1,559
  `it`/`test` openers before `.each` expansion). Each cites its successor or
  `dead:` in §11.1 (P4). The unit-test totals fall accordingly; the
  integrator re-measures them (§8 step 2) and the baseline reds stay exactly
  the HANDOFF §4 ones.
- `translate-domain-error.spec.ts` «falls back to the raw code for an unknown
  code» (`{ code: 'totally_made_up_code' }`) — `dead:` the `{ code }` arm; its
  rule lives in «falls back to the raw errorCode for an unknown struct».
- The dotted half of «reads an ecs-core struct in bg, and the dotted key still
  answers for the legacy class» (`time.invalid_date_time`) — `dead:` with the
  key; the SCREAMING `TIME_INVALID_DATE_TIME` assertion above it stays.

## 6. The grep gate — runbook §4.8 "Phase 4b · the deletion"

P9 adds it to the runbook; it runs after §4.0's preamble (its `gate`/`review`:
`git grep --untracked -nI`, ERROR on an exit above 1) and after §4.2–§4.5 and
§4.7. NO `\b` (git 2.45's ERE on macOS never matches it). Every gate is proved
by a known-bad probe (a scratch line that must FAIL) and the tree (must be
`ok`) before the status line is written.

```bash
FAILED=0
command -v gate >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'ERROR the §4.0 preamble is not loaded'; FAILED=1; }
# History lives in docs/** only. CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md name the deleted libs in their REMOVED
# sentences (reviewed at the end, not gated).
HISTORY=(':!docs/**' ':!CLAUDE.md' ':!AGENTS.md')
# 1. The legacy libraries are gone.
[ ! -e libs/application ] && [ ! -e libs/domain ] \
  && echo 'ok    4b · the legacy libraries are gone' || { echo 'FAIL  4b · the legacy libraries are gone'; FAILED=1; }
# 2. No legacy alias anywhere: code, specs, tsconfigs, eslint configs, rules, mjs, json, .nxignore.
gate '4b · legacy aliases' -E '@creativo/(application|domain)/' -- . "${HISTORY[@]}"
# 3. No legacy path, kernel or relative project reference — comments included (4B-9).
gate '4b · legacy paths' -E 'libs/(application|domain)([^A-Za-z0-9_-]|$)|domain/kernel|"(\.\./)+(application|domain)/[a-z-]+/' \
  -- . "${HISTORY[@]}"
# 3b. The same paths cited WITHOUT the libs/ prefix (added 2026-10-23, OQ 12). On the tree before
#     R1 it lists exactly booking-flow.system.ts:266, busy.system.ts:87, booking-cart.system.ts:310,
#     busy.system.spec.ts:39 — the known-bad run.
gate '4b · legacy paths without libs/' -E '(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_@-])(application/(accounts|booking|catalog|engagement|governance|identity|notifications|shared)|domain/(accounts|catalog|engagement|governance|identity|kernel|models|notifications|scheduling))([^A-Za-z0-9_-]|$)' \
  -- . "${HISTORY[@]}"
# 3c. No code promises a Phase 4 deletion any more (added 2026-10-23). "until Phase 4a/4b" (past
#     tense) is allowed. Before R5 it lists exactly visit-ecs.shim.ts:28 — the known-bad run.
gate '4b · no promise of a Phase 4 deletion' -E '\(Phase 4\)|until Phase 4([^ab]|$)|Phase 4 deletes' -- apps libs tools
# 4. The tag is retired.
gate '4b · type:application' -F 'type:application' -- . "${HISTORY[@]}"
# 5. No luxon / dinero: no import (the lint bans keep their names as retired guards), no
#    dependency, no lockfile entry.
gate '4b · luxon/dinero imports' -E "(from |import\(|require\()'(luxon|dinero\.js)'" -- apps libs tools
gate '4b · luxon/dinero in the lockfile' -iE 'luxon|dinero' -- pnpm-lock.yaml
node -e '
  const p = require(process.cwd() + "/package.json");
  const all = { ...p.dependencies, ...p.devDependencies, ...p.optionalDependencies, ...p.peerDependencies };
  const bad = ["luxon", "@types/luxon", "dinero.js"].filter((n) => Object.hasOwn(all, n));
  for (const n of bad) console.log("package.json: " + n);
  process.exit(bad.length ? 1 : 0);
' && echo 'ok    4b · package.json lists no luxon/dinero' || { echo 'FAIL  4b · package.json lists no luxon/dinero'; FAILED=1; }
# 6. The { code } arm and translateFunctionsError are gone (CodedError's type keeps multi-line
#    calls honest; this catches the one-line ones and the retired names).
gate '4b · translateFunctionsError' -wE 'translateFunctionsError|FunctionsErrorDetails|UNKNOWN_ERROR_KEY' -- apps libs tools
gate '4b · the i18n lib off firebase and the code arm' -E "firebase/functions|readonly code: string" -- libs/infrastructure/i18n/src
gate '4b · { code } arguments' -E 'translateDomainError\([^)]*\{[[:space:]]*code[[:space:]]*[:,}]' -- apps libs
# 6b. No legacy-dotted error or log payload (added 2026-10-23). The prefixes are the legacy wire
#     domains; journal codes (`points.rule`, `tier.changed`, …) and the synthetic `x.y` are not
#     matched. Before R2 it lists exactly R2's eight lines — the known-bad run.
gate '4b · legacy-dotted error payloads' \
  -E "code: '(booking|identity|accounts|scheduling|time|money|catalog|engagement|governance|phone)\.[a-z_.]+'" \
  -- apps libs tools
# 6c. No feature-level alias of a dictionary member (added 2026-10-23, R7, OQ 14). Word-bounded
#     (-w): the successor values ACCOUNTS_UPLOAD_AVATAR_UPLOAD_FAILED / …_REMOVE_FAILED contain the
#     alias names as substrings. On e2d7a8f it lists 15 lines — the known-bad run.
gate '4b · feature-level dictionary aliases' -wE 'PROFILE_WRITE_FAILED|AVATAR_(UPLOAD|REMOVE)_FAILED' -- apps libs
# 7. No dotted errors key in either catalogue, and no reader of one.
node -e '
  let bad = 0;
  for (const lang of ["en", "bg"]) {
    const { errors } = require(process.cwd() + `/libs/infrastructure/i18n/src/assets/i18n/${lang}.json`);
    for (const key of Object.keys(errors)) if (!/^[A-Z]/.test(key)) { console.log(`${lang}.json errors.${key}`); bad++; }
  }
  process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);
' && echo 'ok    4b · no dotted errors key' || { echo 'FAIL  4b · no dotted errors key'; FAILED=1; }
# The leading class keeps file names (`booking-errors.data.ts`, `*.errors.ts`) out. On e2d7a8f it
# lists exactly the 13 sites P0/P1/P2/P5 rewrite (comments included) — the known-bad run.
gate '4b · dotted errors reads' -E "(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_.-])errors\.[a-z][A-Za-z_]*[.'\"\`]" -- apps libs ':!**/*.md'
gate '4b · retired dotted constants' -F -e "'accounts.upload_avatar" -e "'accounts.remove_avatar" \
  -e "'accounts.update_profile" -e "'identifier_missing'" -e "'booking.gateway.failed'" -e 'identity.phone.' \
  -e "'booking.transition.seat_resolved'" \
  -e 'identity.identifier.invalid' -e 'identity.birth_date.invalid' -e 'identity.otp_code.invalid' \
  -e 'booking.reschedule.carried_seat_unplaceable' -- apps libs \
  ':!libs/ecs-core/src/primitives/legacy-wire-successors.spec.ts' \
  ':!libs/ecs-core/src/primitives/pipeline-errors.data.spec.ts'   # wire ledger labels (legacy code → successor), no i18n read
gate "4b · the generic 'unknown' constant" -E "= 'unknown';" -- libs/features libs/infrastructure
gate "4b · 'repository_failure' as an i18n code" -F "code: 'repository_failure'" -- apps libs
# 8. The graph: no project rooted under the legacy trees, so no functions → legacy edge.
GRAPH4B="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/phase-4b-graph.json"
if pnpm nx graph --file="$GRAPH4B" >/dev/null 2>&1 && node -e '
  const { nodes } = require(process.argv[1]).graph;
  const legacy = Object.keys(nodes).filter((n) => /^libs\/(application|domain)\//.test(nodes[n].data.root));
  for (const n of legacy) console.log(`nx graph: ${n}`);
  process.exit(legacy.length ? 1 : 0);
' "$GRAPH4B"; then echo 'ok    4b · no legacy project in the graph'; else echo 'FAIL  4b · no legacy project in the graph'; FAILED=1; fi
# 9. The root config carries the port/adapter blocks and the token guard (4B-7). Probe paths
#    need not exist; an infrastructure path proves the ROOT installs it (no project block there).
#    The icons probe runs FROM its project (ESLint 9 resolves only the cwd's config, and Nx lints
#    each project from its root) and proves no nested config cancels the root blocks: libs/ui/icons
#    turns the rule off on its other .ts files. It FAILS before R8 — the known-bad run. A probe is
#    `<cwd>|<file relative to it>`; severity 0 ('off' keeping stale options — measured: the icons
#    probe keeps `ClassDeclaration` at severity 0) is a FAIL. NOT `pnpm exec`: it runs from the
#    workspace root whatever the cwd, so the nested config would never load.
ROOT4B="$PWD"
for probe in '.|libs/infrastructure/clock/src/lib/probe.adapter.ts' '.|libs/features/probe/src/lib/probe.port.ts' \
  'libs/ui/icons|src/lib/probe.data.ts'; do
  dir="${probe%%|*}"; file="${probe#*|}"
  if (cd "$dir" && node "$ROOT4B/node_modules/eslint/bin/eslint.js" --print-config "$file" 2>/dev/null) | node -e '
    let s = ""; process.stdin.on("data", (d) => (s += d)).on("end", () => {
      const rules = JSON.parse(s).rules ?? {};
      const entry = rules["no-restricted-syntax"] ?? [];
      const on = entry[0] === 2 || entry[0] === "error";
      const syntax = JSON.stringify(entry);
      const ok = on && syntax.includes("InjectionToken") && syntax.includes("ClassDeclaration");
      const port = !process.argv[1].endsWith(".port.ts") || JSON.stringify(rules["creativo/no-sliced-ports"] ?? []).includes("anyType");
      process.exit(ok && port ? 0 : 1);
    });' "$file"; then echo "ok    4b · root ECS blocks on $dir/$file"; else echo "FAIL  4b · root ECS blocks on $dir/$file"; FAILED=1; fi
done
grep -q 'ECS_ROOT_SUFFIXES' eslint.config.mjs tools/lint-rules/*.mjs \
  && { echo 'FAIL  4b · ECS_ROOT_SUFFIXES retired'; FAILED=1; } || echo 'ok    4b · ECS_ROOT_SUFFIXES retired'
# 10. The constitution mirror.
cmp -s CLAUDE.md AGENTS.md && echo 'ok    4b · CLAUDE.md = AGENTS.md' || { echo 'FAIL  4b · CLAUDE.md = AGENTS.md'; FAILED=1; }
review '4b · the constitution names the deleted libs only as removed' -nE 'libs/(application|domain)|Phase 4 deletes|until Phase 4' -- CLAUDE.md AGENTS.md
# 11. firestore.rules is a test input of the projects whose specs parse it (moved from the deleted
#     domain-accounts, P6) — otherwise a rules-only change serves the parity specs from cache.
for proj in ecs-core functions; do
  if pnpm nx show project "$proj" --json 2>/dev/null | node -e '
    let s = ""; process.stdin.on("data", (d) => (s += d)).on("end", () => {
      const t = JSON.parse(s).targets ?? {};
      process.exit(JSON.stringify(t.test?.inputs ?? []).includes("{workspaceRoot}/firestore.rules") ? 0 : 1);
    });'; then echo "ok    4b · firestore.rules is a $proj test input"; else echo "FAIL  4b · firestore.rules is a $proj test input"; FAILED=1; fi
done
# 12. The design-research specs that still link the deleted trees carry the deletion banner (4B-10).
review '4b · design-research files citing the deleted trees (each must carry the banner)' -lE 'libs/(application|domain)' -- docs/design-research
[ "$FAILED" = 0 ] && echo 'phase-4b gate: PASS' || echo 'phase-4b gate: FAIL'
```

If gate 9's selector names differ (`ClassDeclaration` is the class guard's
selector root in `ecs-syntax.mjs`; check `CLASS_GUARD`), the integrator fixes
the probe, not the rule. If `eslint --print-config` refuses a missing path,
the probe uses the Node API (`new ESLint().calculateConfigForFile`).

**Runbook §4.2–§4.7 after the deletion** (P9). `git grep` on a MISSING
positive pathspec exits 1 (`ok`) — such a gate proves nothing. Rewrite:

## 8. Integration and gates

0. **Since 2026-10-23** waves 0–3 are assembled in the working tree; the
   integrator now assembles wave 4 (R1–R4, R4 only if approved) and runs
   §4.R "Wave 5", which re-runs the steps below on the final tree.
1. **Assemble** waves 0 → 3 in order (P0; P1–P4; P5, P6, P8, P9; P7). After
   P6: `pnpm nx reset`, `pnpm nx sync`, `pnpm nx sync:check` (a stale daemon
   or cache can still list the 17 projects). **After P7** (P9 wrote in wave 2,
   before the config it describes was final): diff `module-boundaries.md`'s
   arrows and cages against P7's final `depConstraints` and
   `no-restricted-imports` blocks, fix the doc on any difference (§11.4),
   check P7's `scope:backend` pointer names P9's heading, and dry-run §4.8
   gate 9 (known-bad probe, then the tree) — it cannot pass before P7.
2. **The full gate of HANDOFF §4**, once, then each red project alone:
   `sync:check`; `verify-tools:layering --skip-nx-cache`;
   `run-many -t typecheck --skip-nx-cache` (only `patterns` red); `run-many -t lint`;
   `run-many -t test --parallel=3` (only gauge ×4, visit-pills ×1,
   visits-page.presenter «strikes an hour that did not happen» ×1; re-run
   `staff-dashboard`, `staff-client-card`, `marketing-landing` alone on a
   timeout); `nx test ecs-core --coverage --skip-nx-cache` and
   `nx test functions --skip-nx-cache` (P3/P4 ran ecs-core's tests in wave 1,
   BEFORE P9's `firestore.rules` comment edit and P6's input move — a cached
   result would never parse the edited rules); `nx typecheck seed-tools`;
   `nx build web`; `pnpm run verify:bundle` (record the initial size; it may
   shrink with i18n's `firebase/functions` gone). **Re-measure** the unit-test
   totals per project (they fall by the 169 legacy files) and record them in §8.1.
3. **The functions deps re-record** (after P7, the last config change):
   `pnpm nx build functions --skip-nx-cache`; read
   `dist/apps/functions/package.json` — expected `firebase-admin`,
   `firebase-functions`, `libphonenumber-js`, `rxjs`; then
   `node tools/verify/p0/check-functions-deps.mjs --record` (the integrator
   owns `functions-deps.baseline.json`), then the check WITHOUT `--record`
   (must pass, the forbidden-key half included). The commit message says why:
   "dinero.js leaves with the money spec's cross-check (4B-6, 4B-8); rxjs
   stays (4a §10.1)". **The end-of-3b assertion:** §4.8 gate 8 (no legacy
   project, hence no functions → legacy edge) + the forbidden-key check (no
   `@angular/*`, `luxon`, `dinero.js` in the dist).
4. **Grep gates**, under bash: §4.0 preamble; §4.2–§4.5 as P9 rewrote them;
   §4.7; §4.8 — each new gate dry-run against a known-bad probe first.
5. **Emulator journeys, ONE AT A TIME:** `pnpm run verify:p0` (all proved,
   check (f) the re-recorded deps); `pnpm run test:emulator` (65 + 255,
   unchanged — `firestore.rules` changed in comments only, still re-run);
   `pnpm run e2e:web` (exactly 19 failed — 6 `*-screenshots`,
   `landing.spec.ts:121`, 12 `client-card-glance` at `:343`/`:686` — 41
   passed; `appointments.spec.ts:152` alone with `--repeat-each=3` if it flakes).
6. **Dev-stack smoke** (`pnpm run seed:dev`, `pnpm run dev`; staff sign-in per
   the preview note): `/book` to the review step; `/account` and the profile
   sheet; the staff day (agenda, a visit sheet); the admin rhythm and the
   loyalty admin pages — each opens with no console error. Formerly-dotted
   families, same words (bg, then en): (a) onboarding phone field — a too-short
   number shows «Този номер изглежда твърде кратък — например …» with the
   country's example; (b) with the Functions emulator STOPPED: the `/auth`
   OTP request shows «Възникна грешка — моля, опитайте отново.» (unless the
   transport answers a server code), an appointment cancel shows «Нещо се
   обърка. Пробвай пак.», a profile save shows «Не успяхме да запазим това —
   моля, опитайте отново.», an admin rhythm save shows the toast «Възникна
   проблем от наша страна — моля, опитайте отново.», and the `/account`
   client-account read failure (Firestore emulator stopped, or the read
   refused) renders «Възникна проблем от наша страна — моля, опитайте
   отново.» — the reader exercised, not only its catalogue entry; (c) the
   families not provoked live (avatar upload/remove failure, identifier
   missing, seat resolved): fetch `/assets/i18n/bg.json`
   and `en.json` from the running app and compare each §2 successor with the
   §2 words (a console one-liner, exact equality). Record each check in §8.1.
7. **Records:** §8.1 (what landed, the integrator's fixes and rulings, the
   gate numbers, the smoke), §11; HANDOFF §2/§3.3/§4 results; the runbook §4.8
   status line; the out-of-repo memory note `project_ecs_migration_status`
   (the user's auto-memory) only if the session may write it.

   with the stack). Nothing red beyond the baseline, so no code fix. The
   out-of-repo memory note `project_ecs_migration_status` was refreshed.

## 9. Risks

1. **A successor with different words.** `errors.unknown` ≠ `PIPELINE_UNKNOWN`
   (dash vs full stop); `errors.repository_failure` ≠ `REPOSITORY_*`. Mitigation:
   §2 copies verbatim; P0's equality one-liner; P5's permanent pin; smoke (c).
2. **A dictionary value that changes a classification.** `PipelineErrorCode`,
   `RepositoryErrorCode`, `StreakPublishErrorCode` are enumerated in production
   (`PIPELINE_CODES`, `STORE_FAILURES`, `PUBLISH_REFUSALS`) — 4B-4 forbids
   adding to them. The four extended dictionaries are enumerated only in specs
   and by `EDGE_WIRE`'s type (4B-5).
3. **The seat-resolved trap.** A `<serverErrorCode>.seat_resolved` key would be
   picked up by `wordPipelineError` for `move.system.ts`' `invalidInput('seat_resolved')`.
   Mitigation: the flat `BOOKING_TRANSITION_SEAT_RESOLVED`.
4. **A missed reader.** `booking.gateway.failed` has FOUR readers (one a direct
   translate with a default parameter), and a WRONG constant is as bad
   (`AccountsErrorCode.RepositoryFailure` and `UpdateProfileRepositoryFailure`
   carry different words). Mitigation: §1.3 census; §6 gate 7's reader gates;
   and — because on `e2d7a8f` no spec rendered the client-account read
   failure, the three admin-rhythm `repository_failure` sites, `refuse()`'s
   default key or the onboarding identifier-missing/generic codes — the new
   pins of §5.3, written green on today's tree BEFORE each re-point, so they
   prove the words across it; plus smoke 6(b), which exercises the
   client-account reader live, not only its catalogue entry.
5. **Lost test inputs.** Deleting `domain-accounts` drops the only
   `{workspaceRoot}/firestore.rules` test input; the parity specs would then
   run from cache on a rules-only change. Mitigation: P6 (0) moves it to
   ecs-core and functions in the deleting edit; §4.8 gate 11; §8 step 2
   runs both test targets with `--skip-nx-cache`.
6. **Raw-key specs.** Specs with an empty test catalogue pin the raw code
   (`client-onboarding.spec.ts:394`, `staff-dashboard.spec.ts:2193/2196`);
   they move with their re-point (P1, P2), never weakened.
7. **Vacuous gates.** `git grep` on a deleted positive pathspec says `ok`.
   Mitigation: §6's rewrite table and §4.8's `[ ! -e ]`, graph and catalogue
   checks.
8. **The every-file token guard.** At the root it would flag 67 lawful sites;
   4B-7 installs it on the suffix blocks only (0 hits measured).
9. **A weakened money property.** Mitigation: 4B-6; P3 records the case count
   before/after; coverage of `money.data.ts` unchanged.
10. **The baseline recorded too early** (a config edge growing the dist, or
    dinero still scanned). Mitigation: §8 step 3 runs after P7; the
    forbidden-key check refuses to record a forbidden key.
11. **Stale Nx state** lists the deleted projects. Mitigation: `nx reset` +
    `nx sync` after P6; §4.8 gate 8 on a fresh graph.
12. **The ledger built after the files are gone.** Mitigation: P4 is wave 1;
    P6 waits for its §11.1.
13. **Bundle composition shift** when `firebase/functions` leaves i18n.
    Only a shrink is expected (`FORBIDDEN_IN_INITIAL` is a deny-list); record
    the new size.
14. **Constitution edits refused.** If the user declines 4B-11's edits,
    `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` keep their "until Phase 4" clauses; §4.8's review
    lists them and §8.1 records the decision (the gate does not fail on it).
15. **Lockfile churn.** `pnpm install` may bump unrelated resolutions.
    Mitigation: P6 checks the lockfile diff touches only the three packages;
    otherwise it reverts the lockfile by the inverse edit and re-runs with the
    same pnpm version.
16. **Redoing landed work** (added 2026-10-23). P0–P9 are already in the
    working tree; a package that re-applies one would double-delete or
    re-word twice. Mitigation: §4.R's "already holds" list; wave 4 owns only
    the R1–R4 files.
17. **Gaming gate 7.** Dropping only the `errors.` prefix from R1's comments
    passes the grep but keeps the retired key in code. Mitigation: R1 cites
    the §2 row instead.
18. **The deps baseline recorded before the last config change, or not at
    all.** verify:p0 check (f) is red today. Mitigation: wave 5 step 4 runs
    after R1–R8 on a fresh `--skip-nx-cache` build; R8 is the only wave-4
    lint-config change (a re-export through `#eslint-config` and an
    `ignores`, no new import of `tools/lint-rules`, so no graph edge), and
    it lands before the re-record.
19. **The plan or the deletions left out of the commit.** This plan is
    untracked and the 621 deletions unstaged; a partial `git add` breaks the
    runbook/HANDOFF links or leaves the index listing the dead trees.
    Mitigation: wave 5 step 7.
20. **Agent configuration changed without the owner.** Mitigation: R4 runs
    only on the user's own yes (OQ 11); "please continue" relayed through a
    workflow is not that yes.
21. **A config probe that cannot see the nested config** (added
    2026-10-23). ESLint 9 resolves only the cwd's `eslint.config.mjs`, and
    `pnpm exec` runs from the workspace root whatever the cwd, so a probe
    run from the root (or through `pnpm exec`) passes on `libs/ui/icons`
    even while its `'off'` cancels the blocks, and the cancelled rule keeps
    its stale options at severity 0. Mitigation: gate 9 runs each probe from
    its project dir with the repo's `eslint.js` and requires severity
    `error` (dry-run 2026-10-23: the icons probe FAILS on today's tree, the
    two root probes pass).
22. **A recovered case that never ran red** (R5). A copied table that
    imports the wrong subject passes vacuously. Mitigation: R5 red-proves
    each describe once and records its counts.

## 10. Open questions (owner) — with the default this plan applies

1. **Successors for `repository_failure`.** Default: two domain codes,
   `ACCOUNTS_REPOSITORY_FAILURE` (client account) and
   `ENGAGEMENT_REPOSITORY_FAILURE` (admin rhythm), same words. Alternative:
   reuse `IDENTITY_REPOSITORY_FAILURE` (identical words, which 4a ruled the
   wire successor of the code `repository_failure`) for both — fewer codes,
   wrong domain prefix.
2. **Generic call-failure copy.** Default: `IDENTITY_CALL_FAILED` and
   `BOOKING_CALL_FAILED` (each surface keeps its own generic key, 4A-5).
   Alternative for booking only: reuse `PIPELINE_UNKNOWN` (identical words).
3. **Web-only codes in `EDGE_WIRE`.** Default: inert `internal` rows (4B-5).
   Alternative: a ruling that a web-only dictionary is exempt from the edge's
   "every dictionary" totality (an `edge.spec.ts` change).
4. **The retired-package lint bans.** Default: keep `luxon`/`dinero.js` in
   `LAYER_IMPORT_RESTRICTIONS` and the functions restrictions (stricter).
5. **`no-sliced-ports` strict mode.** Default: add the root `**/*.port.ts`
   block AND keep ecs-core's `src/ports/**` (moving would narrow coverage).
6. **i18n firebase ban.** Default: re-instated (P5) — a tightening.
7. **Constitution and rules edits** (`CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`,
   `.claude/rules/ecs-ports.md`). Default: P9 prepares them; applied only on
   the user's own approval (4B-11).
8. **`rxjs` in the functions dist.** Unchanged default from 4a §10.1: accept.
9. **Provenance form.** Default: keep the trail with the legacy BASENAME and
   a pointer to §11.1, never the path (4B-9). Alternative: delete the
   provenance lines outright (§11.1 alone carries the trail).
10. **`docs/migration/goals/*.md`.** ~~Default: untouched~~ — **re-decided
    2026-10-23:** a one-line banner on `README.md` and on each goal file that
    cites a deleted tree or `domain-model.md` (00, 02, 03, 08), because 4B-10
    calls them "bannered" and their exit gates grep deleted paths (a reader
    running one would get a vacuous `ok`). Docs only (R3). Alternative:
    untouched, and 4B-10's wording corrected instead.
11. **The constitution and rules edits after a "please continue".** The
    relayed user message that resumed 4b is not approval of an
    agent-configuration edit (4B-11). Default: R4 is proposed to the user
    with its exact text and applied only through their own approval; until
    then §8.1 records "pending", HANDOFF's two TODOs say so, and §4.8 gate 10
    reviews (never fails on) the lines.
12. **Gate 3's blind spot.** Gate 3 needs a `libs/` prefix, so four
    provenance comments citing `application/booking/…` without it passed.
    Default: add gate 3b (§6) with the no-prefix ERE; on the current tree it
    lists exactly the four R1 sites (the known-bad run), after R1 none.
13. **The legacy-dotted `{ code }` payloads left in code** — not one but
    eight lines in four specs (`fake-projection-firestore.spec.ts`,
    `firestore-tx.adapter.spec.ts`, `console-telemetry.adapter.spec.ts`,
    `test-support.spec.ts`), plus `telemetry.port.ts`' doc claiming pipelines
    log `{ code }`. Default: each becomes the flat `{ errorCode }` of its 4a
    successor read from the dictionary member (R2), gated by §6 gate 6b — no
    double reads the error, so no outcome moves; `test-support.spec.ts`'
    synthetic `{ code: 'x.y' }` of the generic unwrap helpers stays.
14. **Feature-level aliases of dictionary members** (`PROFILE_WRITE_FAILED`,
    `AVATAR_UPLOAD_FAILED`, `AVATAR_REMOVE_FAILED`, kept by P1 because they
    had importers). Default: delete them, every importer reads
    `AccountsErrorCode.*` directly (R7) — one name per code, as P1 already
    did for the module-private aliases. Alternative: keep them and record
    why in §8.1.
15. **Undecorated classes outside the Angular row** (`VisitClock`,
    `CardClock`, `EdgeScroller`, `FakeFirestore`, `FixtureLoader`). None is
    new and none is legacy-tree code; converting them changes presenters and
    test doubles outside 4b's delete-and-reword scope. Default: carried
    forward and named in HANDOFF and §8.1 — the clocks to the view-model
    pass, `EdgeScroller` as a DOM driver of the UI (Angular row in
    substance), the two test doubles to a test-support pass (a fake of an
    SDK or a Transloco interface) — so §0's "REMOVED" stays accurate about
    the 3-tier shape and the plan says why the class guard stays
    suffix-scoped (§3). Alternative: convert them in 4b (out of scope).
    **Closed 2026-10-23** by the UI residue cleanup (`ui-residue-cleanup.md` UR-7/UR-8): the production classes (these three plus `FrameFullscreen` and `ConsoleUiIconReporter`) are factories, the two test doubles stay under the test-support ignores of `creativo/no-undecorated-class`, which now covers every production file (HANDOFF §3.3).

## 11. The case ledgers

### 11.1 Deleted legacy specs → successors (P4 fills; one row per file, 169)

Pre-filled by the plan: `libs/domain/accounts/src/lib/user-role.rules-parity.spec.ts`
→ `libs/ecs-core/src/components/role.rules-parity.spec.ts`; its project's
`firestore.rules` test input moved to `libs/ecs-core/project.json` (and
`apps/functions/project.json` for `caller-roles.spec.ts`) by P6 (0).

Filled by P4 on the `e2d7a8f` tree, the legacy libraries still in place. One
row per legacy spec file — 169: 157 `*.spec.ts` and 12 compile-only
`*.type-spec.ts`. The figure in parentheses is the file's `it`/`test` openers
(``grep -cE '^\s*(it|test)(\.each[^(]*|\.skip|\.only|\.todo)?\s*[(`]'``, an
`.each` table counted once): 1,591 in all. A successor path with no `libs/` or
`apps/` prefix is under `libs/ecs-core/src/`; «…» quotes a case title (the
successor's, or the legacy case's where the row names what died), and every
quoted title was checked against the spec the row cites. Sources, in order:
phase-4a §11.3 (the 4b pre-ledger), the flip plans' ledgers, the blueprint
§7 (the domain and application spec rows) and the ecs-core provenance
headers — harvested before the 4B-9 sweep rewrote them; each header now names
the legacy BASENAME and points here (OQ-9's default).

`dead:` names the legacy mechanic a case pinned, never a live rule: a
`DomainError` subclass or `Success`/`Failure` (Law 7.5), `reconstitute`/
`toDocument` and the tolerant document readers (Law 5, zero-mapper: the
stored body IS the entity), a value class's accessors, equality or
`Object.freeze` (a brand IS its string; Law 1), an id's `generate()` (Law 3:
ids come from the boundary), the `ZonedDateTime`/`CalendarDay`/`TimeSlot`
epoch mechanics (Law 7.3), an `@Injectable` pass-through use case (4A-8), an
`InjectionToken`, an in-memory reference double or a legacy barrel's shape,
a seeded entity's constructor table (catalogue, coupons, tenant — read as
stored), the dormant reward programs (D21) and the contact-change slice
(D12(b)). Departures an earlier ruling made are named in their rows (the DST
fall-back repeat resolves to the EARLIER offset, Law 7.3, 2026-10-10; the
roster and the day windows stay on the wall clock, T1; `OTHER` takes no note
since flip 1; a transient avatar failure retries, flip 1's media cache).

**added:** 6 cases, all in `libs/features/shared/visits/src/lib/wall-clock.spec.ts`
(P4's file), the template layer's money and instant faces — the legacy
kernel's `formatMoney` and `ZonedDateTime.toLocaleString` had no successor
case: «formatCents» ×5 (`format-money.spec.ts`'s five, same amounts, locales
and strings) and «formatInstant» ×1 (`zoned-date-time.spec.ts`). The ecs-core
tiers owed none: every live rule of a legacy domain or application spec
already had its case there.

**Owed outside P4's files — planned as R5 (wave 4, 2026-10-23):** the
template pipe `levelNumeral` (`libs/features/staff/client-card/src/lib/client-card.format.ts`;
the two `.each` tables of the legacy `standing.spec.ts` — no spec pinned it
directly) and the admin rhythm's grouping `rhythmServiceGroupsOf`
(`libs/features/admin/loyalty/src/lib/admin-rhythm/rhythm-service-groups.ts`;
the one case of `list-services-grouped-by-category.use-case.spec.ts`). The
legacy files exist only in git, so R5 recovers them with `git show e2d7a8f:…`;
wave 5 step 6 checks both successors exist before the records are written.
