that went unclaimed», «refuses to leave a terminal state», «only watches while OPEN or MATCHED»; `systems/waitlist.system.spec.ts` «lapses once every day it named is behind us», «is still live on the last day itself» (copied, its comment), «refuses a window that is no span, an empty bag, and stores the rest normalized» (the declaration and the bag carried; nothing in the bag refused), «drops past days and days past the horizon… and caps the rest» (a request that names no days → `SCHEDULING_WAITLIST_NO_DAYS`), «never asks about a shop for an any-shop request — nothing was read» («accepts "any shop"»); «REFUSES an unowned request» → `pipelines/request-waitlist.pipeline.spec.ts` «refuses a visitor who is not signed in before reading anything» and `apps/functions/src/wire/waitlist.wire.spec.ts` «takes the owner from the token, never the payload — a visitor is nobody». `dead:` «round-trips through props» (Law 5)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `libs/domain/scheduling/src/lib/weekly-pattern.spec.ts` (9)                                         | `components/schedule-version.data.spec.ts` «ShiftSegment.validate — a weekly pattern»: «holds SEVERAL ranges on one weekday — the lunch break», «sorts in week order regardless of authoring order», «REJECTS overlapping ranges rather than merging them», «accepts ranges that merely touch — half-open, so they abut», «rejects a backwards or empty range, and a segment with no shop» (a malformed or backwards range; a segment with no location), «sums a nominal week and names its shops once each»; «treats an absent or empty weekday as not worked», «an empty pattern works no days» → `systems/roster.system.spec.ts` «draws nothing on a day the barber is entirely off», and the nominal week's sum (`components/schedule-version.data.spec.ts` «sums a nominal week…» — nothing rostered sums to zero). `dead:` the «round-trips through props» half of «sums a nominal week and round-trips through props» (Law 5)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
